# App icon inventory

Snapshot: 2026-10-15T20:47:43.587Z. Git revision `e62cb882804c0b998b670031a3a4c3d9e17129fd`. The worktree had 57 modified/untracked entries, recorded verbatim in the audit JSON. The scanned source hashes include those local changes. All line/column references identify this snapshot; use the stable canonical ID and occurrence ID to relocate a symbol after edits.

This catalogue covers every first-party icon import/reference found in the scanned production roots, and records the non-library channels separately. Counts distinguish source occurrences from runtime instances. A loop, tooltip or imported plugin can produce arbitrarily many instances; its source call, input binding, conditions and render variants are the inventory unit.

## Scope and coverage

Scanned roots: `src/`, `package-tools/`, `index.html`. 481 production files, including 438 parsed TS/JS files. 407 test/helper/fixture source files were excluded from the product catalogue and are an explicit non-product category. No public directory exists in this checkout.

Canonical IDs describe pictures, such as `icon.lucide.box`; they do not imply an action. Every separate action/state usage has its own `use.*` ID. IDs hash the code path, component, role and expression, without line numbers. Identical repeated expressions also include their ordinal, so editing an identical earlier occurrence can change that ordinal. Different actions sharing a cube, spline, checkmark or cross remain separate rows.

| Channel | Snapshot count |
| --- | --- |
| Canonical library SVG drawings | 89 |
| Distinct imported library export names | 90 |
| Import bindings/aliases | 221 |
| Resolved icon references, including map/call bindings | 291 |
| Expanded shared-renderer variant rows, additional to direct bindings | 210 |
| Dynamic icon/ReactNode slots | 8 |
| Named renderer calls | 80 |
| Shared icon/control component call sites | 82 |
| Local SVG/image/CSS-shape declarations | 16 |
| SVG primitive templates, including model drawings | 86 |
| Programmatic scene markers/helpers | 8 |
| CSS visual/cursor rules | 60 |
| Browser cursor class locations | 24 |
| Native select/disclosure/checkbox/radio locations | 108 |
| Broad glyph/text candidates, explicitly classified below | 773 |
| First-party raster/vector asset files | 1 |

The audit uses TypeScript symbol identity to resolve import aliases, walks JSX and non-JSX references, records dynamic maps and renderer calls, parses CSS rules, and hashes all scanned production files. A separate lexical scan cross-checks icon-package imports, SVG/image openings and generic Icon tags. Asset enumeration and targeted scans for HTML entities, escaped Unicode, createElementNS, font-icon classes, masks/background URLs and Three.js helpers supplement the AST pass. The audit JSON retains full expressions, attributes, conditions and source hashes when the human-readable table abbreviates long code.

## Canonical library icons

Source package: `lucide-react`, declared `^0.555.0`; actual installed package version is recorded here: `0.555.0`. The SVG drawings are third-party definitions. Every first-party import alias and usage is listed below. Type-only LucideIcon imports are contracts, not pictures.

Variant routes:

- CAD tree `icons[feature.kind]` has 17 entries, each with a separate map row above and one `Icon` rendering slot. Plane, sketch, curve, imported, Pad, Pocket, Revolve, Loft, Sweep, Fillet, Chamfer, Shell, Boolean, transform, linear pattern, circular pattern and mirror are distinct semantic uses.
- CAD `toolButton(label, Icon, action, disabled, active, hint)` receives its icon explicitly at every listed call. The shared button labels its action, applies its tooltip and publishes active state.
- Program-line groups select ChevronDown while expanded and ChevronRight while collapsed, independently for static and body groups. The right collapsed dock aliases PanelRightOpen as ExpandIcon.
- `toastIcon(type)` selects CheckCircle2 for success, XCircle for error, AlertTriangle for warning and Info otherwise. Every variant is used at both the transient dismissible toast call and the saved notification-history call listed below.
- `CadContextHelp` draws Info only when `triggerContent` is absent; plugin relations provide `fx`. Its X closes the matching popover. The helper calls below name each topic. ScalarField adds a topic-specific help icon; SelectField does so only when description exists and adds MousePointer2 only when reference picking is available.
- SectionButton renders its supplied icon prop. SlidingTabs renders an optional `tab.icon`; EditorPage supplies PenTool for Editor and Box for Simulation, separately inventoried at their descriptor locations. Button size="icon" is sizing only and does not create a picture.
- StatusDot colour variants are ready/inactive/invalid/unresolved. CAD parameter groups draw a CSS chevron; simulation cutting state uses a green glow. Dynamic plugin input labels and future installed packages reuse the recorded field/help/relation slots rather than introducing first-party SVG definitions.

## Local pictures, graphics, CSS and assets

Full-size user geometry and teaching diagrams are not toolbar icons. They are inventoried here so inline paths, arrowheads, dots or embedded imagery do not disappear from a library-only replacement exercise. Primitive rows below identify each local source template separately, including those returned by drawing helpers outside their eventual SVG parent.

## Browser-provided symbols

Native arrows/checkmarks/radio dots/disclosure markers are browser/OS drawings, not library SVGs. Their locations and names are listed separately, including every shared Select use (with import aliases) and its native template. Summary markers may be suppressed or restyled by CSS; the CAD parameter group uses its explicit CSS chevron above. SelectField call sites above identify the separate fields behind its shared native select template. Number-input steppers, file-input chooser chrome, range thumbs and scrollbars are also browser controls, not first-party icon definitions.

## Boundaries, ambiguities and omission checks

- node_modules and vendor contain third-party library/native definitions and generated distributions. The inventory records the used Lucide SVG definitions, export aliases and first-party references, rather than every unused dependency icon. Loader2 and LoaderCircle resolve to one canonical drawing; both names and all usages remain listed. Three.js canvas helpers are inventoried as scene graphics. Native material meshes are user/model content.
- dist, build outputs, caches, screenshots, Playwright reports, test results, tmp, DXF test subjects, e2e, tests and src test/helper/fixture files are non-product/generated evidence. They were reviewed as categories and are not first-party UI icon locations. The full src fixture count and all production-file hashes are in the audit JSON.
- docs diagrams, SVG examples and screenshots describe the app or geometry; they are documentation content, not app controls. Documentation build scripts may emit Markdown/HTML illustrations or favicons, but the hosted documentation is a separate content site. This product inventory records the app and package-tools entry points. No source icon hidden in a docs import was found.
- Library pictures do not prove semantic suitability. Box currently spans CAD navigation, stock/body objects, Pad/Pocket and Shell; Spline spans Curve, Path and Sweep; Copy spans patterns and Mirror. Each usage remains separately named and located. Plain tool labels cannot tell a designer whether their generic picture is appropriate.
- Source accessibility is recorded, not certified by these tables. Names supplied by a parent/helper are identified. A lack of a separate SVG aria-label can be intentional when its button has a name; unlabeled state-only decorations still require design review. CSS status-dot rules remaining after UI removal are listed as source definitions, not claimed visible controls.
- Exhaustiveness here means source coverage of the first-party channels described above. It does not enumerate future plugin packages, arbitrary user geometry/text, browser-native artwork or all possible runtime data rows.

Independent lexical totals: lucideImports=49, svgOpenings=6, imageOpenings=1, iconSlots=6. Each imported identifier was reconciled against symbol references; the only unused import, if any, is marked beside its canonical definition. SVG/image opening counts agree with AST roots. Icon suffix matching also finds the imported SettingsIcon alias, which is already in the canonical Settings table and therefore is not a generic slot.

Reproduce this snapshot from the repository root:

```text
node scripts/audit-icon-inventory.mjs
node scripts/audit-icon-inventory.mjs --render
```

The first command refreshes the JSON evidence; --render also regenerates this catalogue using reviewed picture descriptions and glyph classifications. It changes only inventory artifacts. Compare production-file hashes and occurrence IDs before using a saved report against a changed worktree. Do not infer completion from a grep count alone.

### Independent coverage reconciliation

The audit fails if these separate lexical counts disagree with the symbol/AST inventory. The independent pass scans import text and JSX opening tags, rather than reusing the AST traversal output.

| Channel | Independent count | Symbol/AST count | Result |

| importBindings | 221 | 221 | Matched |
| directJsxIcons | 249 | 249 | Matched |
| svgRoots | 6 | 6 | Matched |
| imageRoots | 1 | 1 | Matched |
| primitiveTemplates | 86 | 86 | Matched |
